# GitHub topic: market-research

- **Source:** https://github.com/topics/market-research
- **Repositories:** 1,751 public repos (refreshed 2026-11-23)
- **Most common languages:** Python (652), Not set (606), TypeScript (125), JavaScript (120), HTML (111), Jupyter Notebook (79)
- **Captured via:** GitHub search API, every repo tagged `market-research`, refreshed weekly by `tools/refresh_topics.py`. Descriptions are the authors' own, unedited.

Bookmarked alongside the social-media-research-skills post (entry E01). That repo is the most-starred project in this topic.
